# GasGas MC 125 2026: aftermarket parts

4114 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 347 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 1362 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 2405 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
